L0005: Attachments [Session X](https://calendar.google.com/calendar/event?eid=M2hwNHE3ZWZmcm5vbW9hdWxsNTNia3JvZXBfMjAyNjA4MDRUMjMzMDAwWiBsc3RyZWJlbEByaXNlOC51cw)
L0006: Meeting records [Transcript](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1WSQbj0D6QDZq0cOCRD4PfyV9sWtMDtPnUBM7opONp6Q/edit?usp=drive_web&tab=t.1ba70uvh0naj)
L0007: ### **Summary**
L0008: Roleplaying game session debrief covered game mechanics, tactical combat planning, and group survival strategies for upcoming challenges.
L0009: **Gameplay Mechanics And Logistics**
L0010: Players refined character abilities and finalized the construction of a craft for survival. The group opted to participate in the upcoming bonus round challenge instead of evacuating.
L0011: **Combat And Evasion Tactics**
L0012: Combat encounters with aquatic predators necessitated creative distraction strategies using makeshift slingshots and projectiles. Defensive maneuvers successfully allowed for a tactical aerial escape into the forest canopy.
L0013: **Narrative And System Review**
L0014: The team analyzed environmental obstacles and updated character progress milestones within the campaign. Strategic adjustments to game tools ensured effective monitoring of ongoing survival events.
L0015: ### **Decisions**
L0016: ## Aligned
L0017: * **Horde base strategy established** The group decided to pivot the Seven Days to Die strategy to focus on a specialized funnel-based horde base design instead of continuing forward progress.
L0018: * **Transport strategy set to raft** The team decided to prioritize constructing a raft using barrels and ropes for transport to the forest over other travel methods.
L0019: * **Raft design and weaponry strategy established** The craft design strategy is set to utilize grappling hooks for dragging, fuel hoses for slingshots, bamboo reeds for blow darts, and fuel bladders with tar for the balloon.
L0020: * **Defensive warning shot strategy adopted** The defensive policy is set to utilize warning shots with slingshots, petrified seed pods, and root bundles as the primary deterrent against approaching parties instead of direct physical confrontation.
L0021: * **Eight AM positioning strategy finalized** The positioning strategy is set to station the raft 100 yards off-shore on the water at 8 AM.
L0022: * **Bonus round participation strategy confirmed** The participation strategy is set to continue activity through the bonus round beyond the 5 AM evacuation deadline.
L0023: * **Distraction target set to forest** The group decides that the shark distraction lure should be directed toward the forest area, rather than the center, to draw the threat away from their path.
L0024: * **Raft canopy positioning strategy** The group agrees to maneuver and tie off the raft within the tree canopy level to remain obscured while keeping the balloon inflated.
L0025: * **Ignatius renamed to Natty** The group agrees to refer to the character Ignatius as 'Natty' to avoid confusion with the existing character Iggy.
L0026: * **Raft construction strategy initiated** The team commits to constructing a raft utilizing barrels and bungee cords to navigate across the water to the Mazi forest.
L0027: * **Defensive weapon systems design finalized** The team integrates defensive systems into their craft, including slingshots utilizing petrified seed pods, root bundles, and bamboo reed blow darts.
L0028: * **Evacuation positioning and scouting protocol** The group decides to position themselves in the water at 8 AM, using Iggy for underwater scouting to detect potential threats.
L0029: * **Bonus round participation confirmed** The group commits to remaining for the bonus round after the 8 o'clock evacuation deadline.
L0030: * **Character level increase** All characters are advanced to level five for the next session.
L0031: We've **updated the Decisions section** using your feedback.
L0032: Let us know what you think: [Helpful](https://google.qualtrics.com/jfe/form/SV_5bXzKQfylMIhSXc?isHelpful=True&entryPoint=decisions&confid=3o1PvYWzRP5aFRCE2J0sDxITOAIIigIgABgBCA&isGoogler=False) or [Not Helpful](https://google.qualtrics.com/jfe/form/SV_5bXzKQfylMIhSXc?isHelpful=False&entryPoint=decisions&confid=3o1PvYWzRP5aFRCE2J0sDxITOAIIigIgABgBCA&isGoogler=False)

L0057: ### **Details**
L0058: * **Office Logistics**: John Hagey discusses the recent move of their computer setup to the guest room to create a dedicated office space, noting that the new location is quieter and cleaner. To improve home security, John Hagey plans to move hunting gear and ammunition into this room, maintaining a strategic layout for safety ([00:01:10](#00:01:10)).
L0059: * **Gaming Dice Rolls**: The group reflects on recent game mechanics and dice rolls, specifically celebrating a "crit" roll of seven and seven, while acknowledging that other rolls were less effective ([00:02:22](#00:02:22)).
L0060: * **Game Arena Mechanics**: Luke S introduces a new clock mechanic in their Dagger Heart game, where the arena functions as a countdown clock, allowing players to visualize the terrain and potential traps by clicking on specific icons ([00:03:26](#00:03:26)).
L0061: * **Video Game Strategy**: John Hagey details a strategy for "Seven Days to Die" involving the construction of a complex, non-traditional horde base, which includes a funnel and multiple turrets to maximize experience point gains during horde nights without needing to take cover ([00:05:11](#00:05:11)).
L0062: * **Technical Troubleshooting**: Luke S discusses the challenge of automating NPC generation for their campaign, noting that while the macro correctly populates features and descriptions, they are still working to fix issues with automatic hit point and attack generation ([00:06:04](#00:06:04)).
L0063: * **Gameplay Balancing**: The group explores the "recall cost" mechanic in their game, which allows players to spend stress to move cards from their vault to their active loadout, providing a solution for when players fail to prepare specific spells ahead of time ([00:08:10](#00:08:10)).
L0064: * **Communication and Chat Tools**: The participants clarify their organizational structure, realizing they were checking the wrong chat channel because not everyone uses the same platforms for coordination ([00:09:44](#00:09:44)).
L0065: * **Character Canon**: The participants confirm that the character Lomi canonically has one brown eye and one green eye, which is a deliberate detail rather than a mistake ([00:10:38](#00:10:38)).
L0066: * **Scheduling Coordination**: The group coordinates future travel and session dates, with John Hagey planning to drive through Dallas at the end of August to reach Colorado by September 1st ([00:11:42](#00:11:42)).
L0067: * **Campaign Progression**: The group realizes they are currently on session 10 of their campaign, which began in December, and they estimate they are roughly halfway through the planned storyline ([00:13:44](#00:13:44)).
L0068: * **Magnetic Terrain Narrative**: To explain how they collect salvage, Luke Foreman proposes that the friction from the quicksand-like terrain generates static electricity, which powers magnetic clamps that draw metal scrap to them as they travel ([00:18:42](#00:18:42)).
L0069: * **Narrative Distribution**: The group outlines the current positioning of their characters, where Ignatius (Iggy) drops off Brit with Aggie, who has been exploring the local forest ([00:23:53](#00:23:53)).
L0070: * **Forest Environment**: Aggie describes the "Mazizi forest" as an artificial space that mimics their home but lacks a "soul," noting that it is quiet and difficult to navigate without specific knowledge of the area ([00:26:58](#00:26:58)).
L0071: * **First Encounter with Trees**: Iggy experiences a forest for the first time, as they have not previously seen trees, and they accidentally alert a nearby Cyclops to their presence ([00:31:11](#00:31:11)).
L0072: * **Chase Sequence and Mechanics**: A chase ensues where Iggy uses a stamina potion and evasion rolls to escape the Cyclops, taking some damage before successfully reaching the water to escape ([00:37:05](#00:37:05)) ([00:41:10](#00:41:10)).
L0073: * **Development of Phobia**: Due to the Cyclops throwing trees at them during the escape, Iggy develops a lasting phobia of trees, which the group agrees will be a source of stress for the remainder of the game ([00:42:15](#00:42:15)).
L0074: * **Communication and Gathering**: Aggie interacts with the local trees, confirming that other party members passed through, and begins throwing pebbles into the water to signal Iggy’s location ([00:46:07](#00:46:07)) ([00:49:48](#00:49:48)).
L0075: * **Reunion and Renaming**: Iggy reunites with the party, and Luke Foreman suggests renaming Ignatius to "Natty" to distinguish them from the other characters, "Iggy" and "Aggie" ([00:55:50](#00:55:50)).
L0076: * **Final Planning**: The group decides to build a raft using available materials to paddle back across the water to the Mazizi forest to meet up with the rest of the team ([00:56:51](#00:56:51)).
L0077: * **Name Clarification and Inventory Confirmation**: The participants discuss confusion regarding the names Ignatius, Natty, Iggy, and Aggie, agreeing to resolve the naming convention later. They confirm the inventory collected for their build, which includes shovels serving as paddles, hoses, canvases, bamboo reeds, and fuel bladders containing tar ([00:57:36](#00:57:36)).
L0078: * **Crafting Strategy and Materials**: The team plans to build a hot air balloon for the upcoming "bonus round" using the fuel bladders sealed with tar and sulfur crystals to create fire ([00:59:21](#00:59:21)). They also prepare to use coolant hoses and canvases to build slingshots, intending to utilize root bundles and petrified seed pods as projectiles ([00:58:33](#00:58:33)) ([01:00:11](#01:00:11)).
L0079: * **Role Assignments**: Iggy is designated to lead the engineering efforts due to prior experience seeing similar crafts, while Lomi manages weapon system design and construction, with support from Aggie and Brit ([01:02:11](#01:02:11)) ([01:10:07](#01:10:07)).
L0080: * **Success Mechanics and Journaling**: The team executes rolls to determine the success of their construction efforts, aiming for a 15 on their checks. They also manage the use of the "fear" mechanic and update their shared journal with the craft's details ([01:05:23](#01:05:23)).
L0081: * **Defensive Strategy**: To address potential threats, the team prepares slingshots armed with 10 root bundles and 10 petrified seed pods. These are intended as a deterrent to trip or warn off anyone approaching their position without causing lethal harm ([01:11:08](#01:11:08)).
L0082: * **Observations of Other Groups**: Throughout the night, the team notices other groups near the canyon and shoreline ([01:12:28](#01:12:28)). They decide not to initiate combat, choosing instead to keep watch and maintain their position unless they face a direct threat ([01:13:41](#01:13:41)).
L0083: * **Encounter with Real**: Aggie and Brit encounter a person named Real, who warns them that the upcoming test is likely more dangerous than they anticipate and advises them to impress the captains to improve their standing ([01:17:41](#01:17:41)) ([01:19:44](#01:19:44)). Real provides them with a flare gun to use as an emergency signal if needed ([01:21:51](#01:21:51)).
L0084: * **Final Phase Preparations**: With the 5 AM bell, the team enters the final phase of prep before the 8 AM event ([01:23:51](#01:23:51)). They decide to position their raft in the center of the water, away from the shores, to maximize their field of vision and potential for safety ([01:25:56](#01:25:56)).
L0085: * **Team Morale and Mindsets**: The group discusses their pre-challenge mental states: Lomi acts as a motivator and foreman, Natty/Ignatius focuses on anticipating obstacles, Iggy is absorbed in engineering tasks, and Brit quietly observes using a compass ([01:30:33](#01:30:33)).
L0086: * **Survival Plan for Bonus Round**: The team confirms their commitment to participating in the 8 AM "bonus round" rather than evacuating. They decide to remain adaptable, focusing on reacting to whatever situation arises rather than attempting to predict every specific obstacle ([01:34:12](#01:34:12)) ([01:36:16](#01:36:16)).
L0087: * **Commencement of the Survival Test**: At 8 AM, the survival test officially begins with an announcement over the PA system ([01:38:47](#01:38:47)). The environmental conditions shift, with a sudden storm and a change in the air's static energy, which feels distinct to Aggie and Brit as they connect with their surroundings ([01:39:59](#01:39:59)).
L0088: * **Appearance of Dagger Sharks**: Armored, bioluminescent "dagger sharks" begin approaching the craft ([01:43:07](#01:43:07)). Iggy identifies these creatures as a significant threat, noting that they are capable of tracking living energy and that they have been responsible for the loss of Iggy's friends in the past ([01:44:11](#01:44:11)) ([01:53:36](#01:53:36)).
L0089: * **Evasive Maneuvers and Inflation**: To escape the sharks and the approaching storm, the team attempts to steer the craft toward the shore using riptides and current knowledge. Simultaneously, they work to inflate the balloons using heat from sulfur crystals, prioritizing movement over combat ([01:47:00](#01:47:00)) ([01:49:49](#01:49:49)).
L0090: * **Damage and Combat Engagement**: During the attempt to ignite the balloon, Iggy is attacked by a shark and sustains minor damage ([01:51:40](#01:51:40)). The team continues to navigate the craft while managing the threat, noting that the sharks act in packs and possess the ability to sense life energy ([01:52:46](#01:52:46)).
L0091: * **Distraction and Creature Creation**: Luke S and Holly Strebel discuss using small, mechanical creations as distractions for hostile entities. They propose using a slingshot to deploy these items away from the vessel to create distance from the sharks ([01:55:21](#01:55:21)).
L0092: * **Map Overview and Environmental Threats**: Luke S provides a map view of the area, noting groups fleeing toward the canyon and forest to avoid an approaching storm head ([01:56:21](#01:56:21)). Participants note that the Lomi location has remained static ([01:57:22](#01:57:22)).
L0093: * **Combat and Healing**: The group discusses the immediate threat of sharks surrounding their position. Luke S confirms Iggy has a "Wild Touch" ability, allowing them to start a fire to aid Ignatius, who is currently struggling ([01:58:32](#01:58:32)).
L0094: * **Fire and Balloon Inflation**: John Hagey rolls to light the fire, using Hope and advantage to succeed ([02:00:12](#02:00:12)). The group describes the inflation of the raft's balloon, noting its unique, non-symmetrical shape ([02:01:55](#02:01:55)).
L0095: * **Evasion Tactics**: The group decides to maneuver away from the water to avoid further shark encounters ([02:01:55](#02:01:55)). Luke Foreman attempts an attack on a shark, though it proves ineffective against the creature's high evasion ([02:04:02](#02:04:02)).
L0096: * **Distraction Strategy**: The participants refine the plan to use slingshots for distraction ([02:05:02](#02:05:02)). They decide to direct the sharks toward the forest and away from their target destination ([02:06:34](#02:06:34)).
L0097: * **Operational Roles**: Responsibility for operating the slingshots is assigned to Aggie and Brit, while Iggy coordinates the distraction strategy ([02:07:33](#02:07:33)).
L0098: * **Narrative Maneuver**: The group describes the raft's successful transition into the air, using seed pods to navigate and shift the balloon's trajectory ([02:13:26](#02:13:26)).
L0099: * **Canopy Strategy**: The team plans to secure the raft within the forest canopy, effectively creating a tree-fort-like structure to stay elevated and hidden ([02:17:49](#02:17:49)). They determine it is best to tie the craft to the trees ([02:18:48](#02:18:48)).
L0100: * **System Clocks**: Luke S implements "clocks" to track the balloon's structural integrity and the group's survival time ([02:20:33](#02:20:33)).
L0101: * **Lore and Mechanical Systems**: The group discusses mechanical tests related to resonators, noting that the current environment lacks the energy levels expected ([02:21:50](#02:21:50)).

L0103: * **Scheduling and Progression**: The group agrees to hold two more sessions before August 10th to wrap up the current narrative arc, scheduling them for August 6th and 9th ([02:26:38](#02:26:38)).
L0104: * **Campaign Future**: The participants express interest in a future "Percy Jackson style" campaign following the conclusion of the current arc ([02:28:34](#02:28:34)).
L0105: * **Personal Updates**: Holly Strebel shares details about moving, while John Hagey discusses the setup of a new gaming space in their home ([02:30:12](#02:30:12)).
L0106: * **Mechanics Review**: The players reflect on their recent dice rolls and the effectiveness of their planning ([00:01:48](#00:01:48)). Luke S explains the use of clocks in Daggerheart and how to view terrain descriptions in the game tools ([00:03:57](#00:03:57)).
L0107: * **Game System Mechanics**: Luke Foreman and Luke S discuss Daggerheart's "Recall" cost mechanic, which allows players to swap cards between the vault and their loadout ([00:08:46](#00:08:46)).
L0108: * **Story Continuity**: Participants clarify lore points, such as Lomi's eye color, and discuss previous session notes ([00:10:19](#00:10:19)).
L0109: * **Search and Salvage**: Luke Foreman explains a plan for searching and salvaging parts using magnetism and static electricity generated by the terrain ([00:18:27](#00:18:27)).
L0110: * **Narrative Debrief**: Luke S describes the group's rendezvous, with Brit and Aggie meeting up at the designated point ([00:25:08](#00:25:08)). Aggie relays their experience in the forest, noting it felt artificial and lacked the "soul" of their home environment ([00:26:58](#00:26:58)).
L0111: * **Iggy's Perspective**: Iggy, lost and facing a cyclops, struggles to navigate the forest and consistently emerges back into the open, drawing closer to the pursuer ([00:32:06](#00:32:06)).
L0112: * **Iggy's Evasion**: Luke S guides the group through Iggy's attempts to evade a cyclops and a pursuer named Sarah. Luke S instructs the participants to roll a D4 to determine Iggy’s positioning, noting that there is a 50-50 chance of landing near the water. Holly Strebel manages Iggy's stamina potion usage and movement rolls. The group confirms Iggy avoids the cyclops’ grasp, though Sarah gives chase ([00:35:33](#00:35:33)).
L0113: * **Iggy's Injury and Stress**: Iggy attempts to escape the pursuit by heading toward the water. During the chase, the cyclops throws a tree at Iggy, causing six damage. Luke S explains that since Iggy has no armor, this results in three hit points of damage, or the option to burn three stress to reduce the severity. Holly Strebel opts to take the stress, establishing that Iggy now has a permanent phobia of trees as a result of the encounter. Iggy successfully reaches the deep water, where they are safe from the immediate pursuit ([00:39:16](#00:39:16)).
L0114: * **Regrouping Strategies**: Aggie and Brit coordinate to locate Iggy, who is hiding in the water. Aggie utilizes an instinct check of 17 to communicate with the surrounding trees, which inform them that Iggy was previously lost in the forest and was being pursued. Aggie decides to signal Iggy by dropping rocks along the shoreline in a rhythmic, methodical pattern to confirm their location. Meanwhile, Brit focuses on assembling materials for their upcoming craft ([00:44:08](#00:44:08)).
L0115: * **Iggy's Reunion**: Following the signal from the pebbles, Aggie and Iggy successfully reunite on the shoreline. Iggy remains suspicious of trees, but Aggie provides emotional support, attempting to explain that the cyclops is responsible for the threats rather than the trees themselves. Luke S confirms the group finishes their canyon run and prepares to coordinate for the next phase of the challenge ([00:51:41](#00:51:41)).
L0116: * **Raft Construction and Team Roles**: Luke Foreman introduces a plan to build a raft to navigate toward the mushroom forest. To avoid confusion with the existing name Iggy, Luke Foreman renames the character Ignatius to "Natty." The group collects various materials, including fuel hoses, canvas, and bamboo reeds. They plan to use these to construct a raft to paddle across the water, utilizing fuel bladders and tar to seal the base ([00:55:57](#00:55:57)).
L0117: * **Weapons and Crafting Success**: The team finalizes their weapon designs, creating slingshots from coolant hoses and canvas patches. These will launch petrified seed pods and myelial root bundles. Luke S requires the group to roll a 15 to succeed in their craft-building check. Despite some initial difficulty, the group achieves a full success, resulting in an upgraded vehicle and defensive capabilities ([01:01:20](#01:01:20)).
L0118: * **Tactical Positioning and Fear Mechanics**: Luke S introduces the use of a "fear" mechanic, which affects the structural integrity of their craft. Throughout the night, the group observes other students moving along the canyon shores. They decide to remain concealed and observe rather than initiating contact, focusing on maintaining their position until the 8:00 a.m. evacuation ([01:07:34](#01:07:34)).
L0119: * **Warning from Real**: At 4:00 a.m., an individual named Real approaches the group and warns them that the upcoming event at 8:00 a.m. is likely to be significantly more dangerous than expected. Real suggests that survival itself will be an impressive feat, though it may provide opportunities for heroics. Before departing, Real provides the group with a flare gun to be used if they need assistance during the event ([01:15:12](#01:15:12)).
L0120: * **Final Preparations for 8:00 a.m.**: The group discusses their final strategy, intending to be on the water, approximately 100 yards from the shore, by 8:00 a.m. They refine their weapon systems, which now include water balloon slingshots and staff-based reed launchers. The group describes the atmosphere as similar to a locker room, with individuals managing their pre-game nerves as they prepare for the final phase ([01:25:29](#01:25:29)).
L0121: * **Ignatius and Britt Update**: John Hagey discusses the status of Ignatius, often referred to as Natty or Iggy, describing their engineering mindset as they prepare for potential obstacles. Sophie Foreman Noone observes that Britt is monitoring a compass, and the group discusses their progress and the state of their makeshift craft ([01:32:18](#01:32:18)).
L0122: * **Bonus Round Decision**: The group confirms their plan to stay for the "bonus round" after the 8:00 o'clock mark, acknowledging that they will need to react to the situation as it develops rather than relying on a fixed plan ([01:34:24](#01:34:24)).
L0123: * **Discussion of Rumors**: Luke S shares information from other members regarding potential dangers in the area. The group discusses whether to trust these reports, ultimately deciding to remain cautious without explicitly choosing a side in the conflicts ([01:35:26](#01:35:26)).
L0124: * **Environmental Changes**: As the group moves their craft to the center of the water, the environment shifts. Luke S notes that arena lights change, a PA announcement plays, and the air becomes charged with ozone and static electricity, indicating a major storm is approaching ([01:36:35](#01:36:35)).
L0125: * **Arrival of Dagger Sharks**: A significant storm front moves in, and the group identifies "dagger sharks"—armored, fast-moving creatures—approaching their position. Iggy shares information about these predators, noting that they are dangerous, tenacious, and capable of hunting in packs ([01:41:17](#01:41:17)).
L0126: * **Navigation and Evasion**: The group initiates "phase two," which involves inflating their balloon-raft to lift off the water. Iggy uses their ability to sense the tide to help navigate, while others focus on rowing to evade the pursuing sharks ([01:45:34](#01:45:34)).
L0127: * **Inflating the Balloon**: Natty attempts to inflate the balloons by lighting a sulfur crystal to generate heat. The group discusses mechanics, with Natty applying a bonus from their "sparking engines" to the roll ([01:49:38](#01:49:38)).
L0128: * **Shark Attack and Damage**: During the inflation process, a dagger shark attacks Natty. Natty takes eight damage but uses an armor slot to reduce the impact, prioritizing their health and well-being ([01:52:18](#01:52:18)).
L0129: * **Shark Behavior and Distraction**: Iggy explains that the sharks track living energy. The group discusses using mechanical tokens or seed pods launched via slingshots to act as decoys, effectively drawing the predators away from their vessel ([01:54:15](#01:54:15)).
L0130: * **Scouting the Area**: The group observes their surroundings and notices other teams fleeing the nearby canyon area. They decide to avoid the center, which is becoming increasingly exposed ([01:57:11](#01:57:11)).
L0131: * **Balloon Inflation Success**: Aggie uses their "wild touch" ability to assist with the fire, which helps Natty successfully inflate the balloons. The craft lifts off the water, providing the group with much-needed altitude ([01:59:03](#01:59:03)).
L0132: * **Combat Maneuvers**: The group attempts to distract the sharks by firing projectile pods into the water. While the initial shots do not immediately stop the predators, they provide enough of a distraction to continue their escape ([02:03:51](#02:03:51)).
L0133: * **Targeting the Forest**: The group discusses their destination and decides to head toward the forest canopy rather than the exposed center. Iggy instructs the group on how to best use the slingshots to ensure the sharks stay occupied with decoys ([02:05:32](#02:05:32)).
L0134: * **Strategic Visualization**: John Hagey presents a clip from "The A-Team" to help the group visualize their strategy for "flying the tank." This helps the players coordinate their maneuvers as they shift the craft toward safety ([02:09:00](#02:09:00)).
L0135: * **Landing and Camouflage**: The group successfully navigates the balloon to the tree line. They reflect on their earlier decision to paint the fuel bladders to match the forest, which now serves as effective camouflage ([02:15:30](#02:15:30)).
L0136: * **System Mechanics**: Luke S establishes clocks to track the balloon's integrity and the duration of the survival event. The group acknowledges that the situation will likely require several hours of vigilance ([02:20:27](#02:20:27)).
L0137: * **Session Scheduling**: The group schedules future sessions for August 6th and August 9th, with a start time of 7:30 p.m., to work toward a narrative conclusion for the current campaign arc ([02:24:35](#02:24:35)).
